									local U, r = X[S], B[J]

									U *= U

									for B = 2, #R do
										local F = R[B]

										if not F then
											continue
										end

										local B = F[p]

										if not B then
											continue
										end

										local H = L(B, P)

										if not H then
											continue
										end

										local B = H[J] - r

										if A(B, B) > U then
											continue
										end

										h(F)
									end
								end
							end)

							AddToggle4(murderer, "Knife Aura", knifeAura)
						end
					end

					do
						local AddSlider2 = murderer.AddSlider
						local knifeRange = v1718(1, "KnifeRange")

						AddSlider2(murderer, "Knife Range", 1, 255, 5, knifeRange)
					end
				end

					local B, V, n, _, _, X, z = t333, t19, v214, spilled.vector3New, spilled.color3, v219, v222
					local U, F, H, S, J, p, P, O, s, e, R, L, h = B[1], B[2], B[3], B[4], B[5], B[6], B[7], B[8], B[9], B[10], B[11], B[12], B[13]

					while V[U] do
						local B, U, A, Q = V[F], V[H], V[S], V[J]

						for F = 2, #z do
							local H = z[F]

							if not H then
								continue
							end

							local F = H[p]

							if not F then
								continue
							end

							local H = X(F, P)

							if not H then
								continue
							end

							H[O] = Q
							H[s] = U
							H[e] = A
							H[R] = B
							H[L] = false
						end

						n(0.25)
					end

					local B, U = color3, vector32

					for r = 2, #z do
						local F = z[r]

						if not F then
							continue
						end

						local r = F[p]

						if not r then
							continue
						end

						local F = X(r, P)

						if not F then
							continue
						end

						F[O] = 1
						F[s] = h
						F[e] = B
						F[R] = U
						F[L] = true
					end
				end)

				AddToggle2(v5656, "Enable Hitbox Expander", hitboxExpander)
